-------------
<span id="wlc-q1"></span>
### Question 1: What is the chief and highest end of man?
Answer: Man’s chief and highest end is to glorify God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q1-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 1">a</a></sup> and fully to enjoy him forever.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q1-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 1">b</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q1-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>a.</strong> Rom. 11:36; 1 Cor. 6:20; 1 Cor. 10:31; Ps. 86:9, 12, <strong>b.</strong> Ps. 73:24-28; John 17:21-23; Ps. 16:5-11; Rev. 21:3-4</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q2"></span>
### Question 2: How <span class="text-variant" data-modern="does">doth</span> it appear that there is a God?
Answer: The very light of nature in man, and the works of God, declare plainly that there is a God;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q2-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 2">c</a></sup> but his Word and Spirit <span class="text-variant" data-modern="alone">only</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">do</span> sufficiently and effectually reveal him <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> men for their salvation.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q2-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 2">d</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q2-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>c.</strong> Rom. 1:19-20; Acts 17:28; Ps. 19:1-3, <strong>d.</strong> 1 Cor. 2:9-10; 1 Cor. 1:20-21; Isa. 59:21</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q3"></span>
### Question 3: What is the Word of God?
Answer: The Holy Scriptures of the Old and New Testament are the Word of God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q3-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 3">e</a></sup> the only rule of faith and obedience.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q3-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 3">f</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q3-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>e.</strong> 2 Tim. 3:16; 2 Pet. 1:19-21; 2 Pet. 3:2, 15-16; Matt. 19:4-5; Gen. 2:24, <strong>f.</strong> Deut. 4:2; Eph. 2:20; Rev. 22:18-19; Isa. 8:20; Luke 16:29, 31; Gal. 1:8-9; 2 Tim. 3:15-16</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q4"></span>
### Question 4: How <span class="text-variant" data-modern="does">doth</span> it appear that the Scriptures are the Word of God?
Answer: The Scriptures manifest themselves to be the Word of God, by their majesty<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q4-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 4">g</a></sup> and purity;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q4-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 4">h</a></sup> by the consent of all the parts,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q4-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 4">i</a></sup> and the scope of the whole, which is to give all glory to God;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q4-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 4">k</a></sup> by their light and power to convince and convert sinners, to comfort and build up believers <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> salvation:<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q4-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 4">l</a></sup> but <span class="text-variant" data-modern="only"></span> the Spirit of God bearing witness by and with the Scriptures in the heart of <span class="text-variant" data-modern="man">man,</span> is <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">alone</span> able fully to persuade it that they are the very Word of God.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q4-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 4">m</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q4-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>g.</strong> Hos. 8:12; 1 Cor. 2:6-7, 13; Ps. 119:18, 129, <strong>h.</strong> Ps. 12:6; Ps. 119:140, <strong>i.</strong> Luke 24:27; Acts 10:43; Acts 26:22, <strong>k.</strong> Rom. 3:19, 27; Rom. 16:25-27; 2 Cor. 3:6-11, <strong>l.</strong> Acts 18:28; Heb. 4:12; James 1:18; Ps. 19:7-9; Rom. 15:4; Acts 20:32, <strong>m.</strong> John 16:13-14; 1 John 2:20, 27; John 20:31</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q5"></span>
### Question 5: What do the Scriptures principally teach?
Answer: The Scriptures principally <span class="text-variant" data-modern="teach">teach,</span> what man is to believe concerning God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q5-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 5">n</a></sup> and what duty God requires of man.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q5-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 5">o</a></sup> WMOB HAT AN UGHT TO ELIEVE CONCERNING GOD

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q5-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>n.</strong> Gen. 1:1; Ex. 34:5-7; Ps. 48:1; John 20:31; 2 Tim. 3:15, <strong>o.</strong> Deut. 10:12-13; 2 Tim. 3:15-17; Acts 16:30-31</p>
</div>
</details>


## WHAT MAN OUGHT TO BELIEVE CONCERNING GOD

<span id="wlc-q6"></span>
### Question 6: What do the Scriptures make known of God?
Answer: The Scriptures make known what God is,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q6-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 6">p</a></sup> the persons in the Godhead,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q6-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 6">q</a></sup> his decrees,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q6-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 6">r</a></sup> and the execution of his decrees.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q6-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 6">s</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q6-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>p.</strong> John 4:24; Ex. 34:6-7; Isa. 40:18, 21-23, 25, 28; Heb. 11:6, <strong>q.</strong> Matt. 3:16-17; Deut. 6:4-6; 1 Cor. 8:4, 6; Matt. 28:19-20; 2 Cor. 13:14, <strong>r.</strong> Acts 15:14-15, 18; Isa. 46:9-10, <strong>s.</strong> Acts 4:27-28</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q7"></span>
### Question 7: What is God?
Answer: God is a Spirit,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 7">t</a></sup> in and of himself infinite in being,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 7">u</a></sup> glory,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 7">w</a></sup> blessedness,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 7">x</a></sup> and perfection;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 7">y</a></sup> all-<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 7">z</a></sup> sufficient, eternal,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 7">a</a></sup> unchangeable,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 7">b</a></sup> incomprehensible,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 7">c</a></sup> everywhere present,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 7">d</a></sup> almighty,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 7">e</a></sup> knowing all things,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 7">f</a></sup> most wise,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 7">g</a></sup> most holy,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 7">h</a></sup> most just,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 7">i</a></sup> most merciful and gracious, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="longsuffering,">long-suffering,</span> and abundant in goodness and truth.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q7-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 7">k</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q7-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>t.</strong> John 4:24, <strong>u.</strong> Ex. 3:14; Job 11:7-9; Ps. 145:3; Ps. 147:5, <strong>w.</strong> Acts 7:2, <strong>x.</strong> 1 Tim. 6:15, <strong>y.</strong> Matt. 5:48, <strong>z.</strong> Ex. 3:14; Gen. 17:1; Rom. 11:35-36, <strong>a.</strong> Ps. 90:2; Deut. 33:27, <strong>b.</strong> Mal. 3:6, <strong>c.</strong> 1 Kings 8:27; Ps. 145:3; Rom. 11:34, <strong>d.</strong> Ps. 139:1-13, <strong>e.</strong> Rev. 4:8; Gen. 17:1; Matt. 19:26, <strong>f.</strong> Heb. 4:13; Ps. 147:5, <strong>g.</strong> Rom. 11:33-34; Rom. 16:27, <strong>h.</strong> 1 Pet. 1:15-16; Rev. 15:4; Isa. 6:3, <strong>i.</strong> Deut. 32:4; Rom. 3:5, 26, <strong>k.</strong> Ex. 34:6; Ps. 117:2; Deut. 32:4</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q8"></span>
### Question 8: Are there more Gods than one?
Answer: There is <span class="text-variant" data-modern="only one,">but</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">one</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">only,</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q8-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 8">l</a></sup> the living and true God.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q8-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 8">m</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q8-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>l.</strong> Deut. 6:4; 1 Cor. 8:4, 6; Isa. 45:21-22; Isa. 44:6, <strong>m.</strong> Jer. 10:10; John 17:3; 1 Thess. 1:9; 1 John 5:20</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q9"></span>
### Question 9: How many persons are there in the Godhead?
Answer: There <span class="text-variant" data-modern="are">be</span> three persons in the Godhead, the Father, the Son, and the Holy <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Spirit;">Ghost;</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q9-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 9">n</a></sup> and these three are one true, eternal God, the same in substance, equal in power and glory; although distinguished by their personal properties.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q9-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 9">o</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q9-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>n.</strong> Matt. 3:16-17; Matt. 28:19; 2 Cor. 13:14, <strong>o.</strong> John 1:1; Gen. 1:1-3; John 17:5; John 10:30; Ps. 45:6; Heb. 1:8-9; Acts 5:3-4; Rom. 9:5; Col. 2:9</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q10"></span>
### Question 10: What are the personal properties of the three persons in the Godhead?
Answer: It is proper to the Father to beget the Son,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q10-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 10">p</a></sup> and to the Son to be begotten of the Father,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q10-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 10">q</a></sup> and to the Holy <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Spirit">Ghost</span> to proceed from the Father and the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Son,">Son</span> from all eternity.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q10-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 10">r</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q10-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>p.</strong> Heb. 1:5-6, 8, <strong>q.</strong> John 1:14, 18, <strong>r.</strong> John 15:26; Gal. 4:6</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q11"></span>
### Question 11: How <span class="text-variant" data-modern="does">doth</span> it appear that the Son and the Holy <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Spirit">Ghost</span> are God equal with the Father?
Answer: The Scriptures manifest that the Son and the Holy <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Spirit">Ghost</span> are God equal with the Father, ascribing <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> them such names,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q11-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 11">s</a></sup> attributes,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q11-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 11">t</a></sup> works,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q11-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 11">u</a></sup> and worship,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q11-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 11">w</a></sup> as are proper to God only.

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q11-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>s.</strong> Isa. 6:3, 5, 8; John 12:41; Acts 28:25; 1 John 5:20; Acts 5:3-4, <strong>t.</strong> John 1:1; Isa. 9:6; John 2:24-25; 1 Cor. 2:10-11, <strong>u.</strong> Col. 1:16; Gen. 1:2, <strong>w.</strong> Matt. 28:19; 2 Cor. 13:14</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q12"></span>
### Question 12: What are the decrees of God?
Answer: God’s decrees are the wise, free, and holy acts of the counsel of his will,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q12-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 12">x</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by which,">whereby,</span> from all eternity, he <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has,">hath,</span> for his own glory, unchangeably foreordained <span class="text-variant" data-modern="whatever">whatsoever</span> comes to pass in time,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q12-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 12">y</a></sup> especially concerning angels and men.

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q12-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>x.</strong> Isa. 45:6-7; Eph. 1:11; Rom. 11:33; Rom. 9:14-15, 18, <strong>y.</strong> Ps. 33:11; Isa. 14:24; Acts 2:23; Acts 4:27-28; Rom. 9:22-23; Eph. 1:4, 11</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q13"></span>
### Question 13: What <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has">hath</span> God especially decreed concerning angels and men?
Answer: God, by an eternal and immutable decree, out of his mere love, for the praise of his glorious grace, to be manifested in due time, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has">hath</span> elected some angels to glory;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q13-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 13">z</a></sup> and in Christ <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has">hath</span> chosen some men to eternal life, and the means <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to this end:">thereof:</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q13-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 13">a</a></sup> and also, according to his sovereign power, and the unsearchable counsel of his own will, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="(by which">(whereby</span> he <span class="text-variant" data-modern="extends">extendeth</span> or <span class="text-variant" data-modern="withholds">withholdeth</span> favor as he <span class="text-variant" data-modern="pleases,) has">pleaseth,)</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">hath</span> passed by and foreordained the rest to dishonor and wrath, to be <span class="text-variant" data-modern="inflicted"></span> for their <span class="text-variant" data-modern="sin,">sin</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">inflicted,</span> to the praise of the glory of his justice.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q13-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 13">b</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q13-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>z.</strong> 1 Tim. 5:21, <strong>a.</strong> Eph. 1:4-6; Eph. 2:10; 2 Thess. 2:13-14; 1 Pet. 1:2, <strong>b.</strong> Rom. 9:17-18, 21-22; Matt. 11:25-26; 2 Tim. 2:20; Jude 4; 1 Pet. 2:8</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q14"></span>
### Question 14: How <span class="text-variant" data-modern="does">doth</span> God execute his decrees?
Answer: God <span class="text-variant" data-modern="executes">executeth</span> his decrees in the works of creation and providence,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q14-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 14">c</a></sup> according to his infallible foreknowledge, and the free and immutable counsel of his own will.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q14-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 14">d</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q14-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>c.</strong> Rev. 4:11; Isa. 40:12-31, <strong>d.</strong> Eph. 1:11; Ps. 148:8; Dan. 4:35; Acts 4:24-28</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q15"></span>
### Question 15: What is the work of creation?
Answer: The work of creation is that <span class="text-variant" data-modern="in which">wherein</span> God <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">did</span> in the beginning, by the word of his power, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="made">make</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">of</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">nothing</span> the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="world">world,</span> and all things <span class="text-variant" data-modern="in it of nothing,">therein,</span> for himself, within the space of six days, and all very good.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q15-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 15">e</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q15-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>e.</strong> Gen. 1:1; Ps. 33:6, 9; Heb. 11:3; Rev. 4:11; Rom. 11:36</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q16"></span>
### Question 16: How did God create angels?
Answer: God created all the angels<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q16-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 16">f</a></sup> spirits,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q16-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 16">g</a></sup> immortal,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q16-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 16">h</a></sup> holy,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q16-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 16">i</a></sup> excelling in knowledge,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q16-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 16">k</a></sup> mighty in power,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q16-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 16">l</a></sup> to execute his commandments, and to praise his name,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q16-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 16">m</a></sup> yet subject to change.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q16-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 16">n</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q16-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>f.</strong> Col. 1:16, <strong>g.</strong> Ps. 104:4, <strong>h.</strong> Matt. 22:30; Luke 20:36, <strong>i.</strong> Matt. 25:31, <strong>k.</strong> 2 Sam. 14:17; Matt. 24:36, <strong>l.</strong> 2 Thess. 1:7, <strong>m.</strong> Ps. 91:11-12; Ps. 103:20-21, <strong>n.</strong> 2 Pet. 2:4</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q17"></span>
### Question 17: How did God create man?
Answer: After God had made all other creatures, he created man male and female;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q17-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 17">o</a></sup> formed the body of the man of the dust of the ground,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q17-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 17">p</a></sup> and the woman of the rib of the man,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q17-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 17">q</a></sup> endued them with living, reasonable, and immortal souls;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q17-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 17">r</a></sup> made them after his own image,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q17-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 17">s</a></sup> in knowledge,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q17-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 17">t</a></sup> righteousness, and holiness;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q17-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 17">u</a></sup> having the law of God written in their hearts,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q17-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 17">w</a></sup> and power to fulfill it,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q17-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 17">x</a></sup> and dominion over the creatures;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q17-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 17">y</a></sup> yet subject to fall.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q17-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 17">z</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q17-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>o.</strong> Gen. 1:27; Matt. 19:4, <strong>p.</strong> Gen. 2:7, <strong>q.</strong> Gen. 2:22, <strong>r.</strong> Gen. 2:7; Job 35:11; Eccl. 12:7; Matt. 10:28; Luke 23:43, <strong>s.</strong> Gen. 1:26-27, <strong>t.</strong> Col. 3:10, <strong>u.</strong> Eph. 4:24, <strong>w.</strong> Rom. 2:14-15, <strong>x.</strong> Eccl. 7:29, <strong>y.</strong> Gen. 1:28; Ps. 8:6-8, <strong>z.</strong> Gen. 2:16-17; Gen. 3:6; Eccl. 7:29</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q18"></span>
### Question 18: What are God’s works of providence?
Answer: God’s works of providence are his most holy,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q18-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 18">a</a></sup> wise,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q18-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 18">b</a></sup> and powerful preserving<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q18-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 18">c</a></sup> and governing<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q18-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 18">d</a></sup> all his creatures; ordering them, and all their actions,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q18-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 18">e</a></sup> to his own glory.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q18-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 18">f</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q18-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>a.</strong> Ps. 145:17; Lev. 21:8, <strong>b.</strong> Ps. 104:24; Isa. 28:29, <strong>c.</strong> Heb. 1:3; Ps. 36:6; Neh. 9:6, <strong>d.</strong> Ps. 103:19; Job 38; Ps. 145:14-16, <strong>e.</strong> Matt. 10:29-31; Gen. 45:7; Ps. 135:6, <strong>f.</strong> Rom. 11:36; Isa. 63:14</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q19"></span>
### Question 19: What is God’s providence towards the angels?
Answer: God by his providence permitted some of the angels, willfully and irrecoverably, to fall into sin and damnation,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q19-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 19">g</a></sup> limiting and ordering that, and all their sins, to his own glory;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q19-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 19">h</a></sup> and established the rest in holiness and happiness;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q19-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 19">i</a></sup> employing them all,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q19-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 19">k</a></sup> at his pleasure, in the administrations of his power, mercy, and justice.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q19-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 19">l</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q19-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>g.</strong> Jude 6; 2 Pet. 2:4; Heb. 2:16; John 8:44, <strong>h.</strong> Job 1:12; Matt. 8:31; Luke 10:17, <strong>i.</strong> 1 Tim. 5:21; Mark 8:38; Heb. 12:22, <strong>k.</strong> Ps. 103:20; Ps. 104:4, <strong>l.</strong> Heb. 1:14; 2 Kings 19:35</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q20"></span>
### Question 20: What was the providence of God toward man in the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> in which he was created?
Answer: The providence of God toward man in the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> in which he was created, was <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">the</span> placing him in paradise, appointing him to <span class="text-variant" data-modern="work">dress</span> it, giving him liberty to eat of the fruit of the earth;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q20-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 20">m</a></sup> putting the creatures under his dominion,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q20-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 20">n</a></sup> and ordaining marriage for his help;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q20-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 20">o</a></sup> affording him communion with himself;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q20-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 20">p</a></sup> instituting the Sabbath;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q20-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 20">q</a></sup> entering into a covenant of life with him, upon condition of personal, perfect, and perpetual obedience,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q20-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 20">r</a></sup> of which the tree of life was a pledge;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q20-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 20">s</a></sup> and forbidding to eat of the tree of the knowledge of good and evil, upon the pain of death.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q20-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 20">t</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q20-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>m.</strong> Gen. 2:8, 15-16, <strong>n.</strong> Gen. 1:28, <strong>o.</strong> Gen. 2:18; Matt. 19:3-9; Eph. 5:31, <strong>p.</strong> Gen. 1:26-29; Gen. 3:8, <strong>q.</strong> Gen. 2:3; Ex. 20:11, <strong>r.</strong> Gen. 2:16-17; Gal. 3:12; Rom. 10:5, <strong>s.</strong> Gen. 2:9; Gen. 3:22-24, <strong>t.</strong> Gen. 2:17; James 2:10</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q21"></span>
### Question 21: Did man continue in that <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state in which">estate</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">wherein</span> God at first created him?
Answer: Our first parents being left to the freedom of their own will, through the temptation of Satan, transgressed the commandment of God in eating the forbidden fruit; and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by this">thereby</span> fell from the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> of <span class="text-variant" data-modern="innocence in which">innocency</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">wherein</span> they were created.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q21-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 21">u</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q21-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>u.</strong> Gen. 3:6-8, 13; Eccl. 7:29; 2 Cor. 11:3</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q22"></span>
### Question 22: Did all mankind fall in that first transgression?
Answer: The covenant being made with Adam as a public person, not for himself only, but for his posterity, all mankind descending from him by ordinary generation,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q22-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 22">w</a></sup> sinned in him, and fell with him in that first transgression.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q22-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 22">x</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q22-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>w.</strong> Acts 17:26; Rom. 3:23, <strong>x.</strong> Gen. 2:16-17; James 2:10; Rom. 5:12-20; 1 Cor. 15:21-22</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q23"></span>
### Question 23: Into what <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> did the fall bring mankind?
Answer: The fall brought mankind into <span class="text-variant" data-modern="a state">an</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">estate</span> of sin and misery.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q23-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 23">y</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q23-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>y.</strong> Gen. 3:16-19; Rom. 5:12; Eph. 2:1; Rom. 3:16, 23</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q24"></span>
### Question 24: What is sin?
Answer: Sin is any <span class="text-variant" data-modern="lack">want</span> of conformity <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to,">unto,</span> or transgression of, any law of God, given as a rule to the reasonable creature.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q24-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 24">z</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q24-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>z.</strong> Lev. 5:17; James 4:17; 1 John 3:4; Gal. 3:10, 12</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q25"></span>
### Question 25: <span class="text-variant" data-modern="What is">Wherein</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">consisteth</span> the sinfulness of that <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state into which">estate</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">whereinto</span> man fell?
Answer: The sinfulness of that <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state into which">estate</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">whereinto</span> man fell, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="consists">consisteth</span> in the guilt of Adam’s first sin,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q25-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 25">a</a></sup> the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="lack">want</span> of that righteousness <span class="text-variant" data-modern="in which">wherein</span> he was created, and the corruption of his nature, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by which">whereby</span> he is utterly indisposed, disabled, and made opposite <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> all that is spiritually good, and wholly inclined to all evil, and that continually;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q25-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 25">b</a></sup> which is commonly called original sin, and from which <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">do</span> proceed all actual transgressions.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q25-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 25">c</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q25-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>a.</strong> Rom. 5:12, 19; 1 Cor. 15:22, <strong>b.</strong> Rom. 3:10-12; Eph. 2:1-3; Rom. 5:6; Rom. 8:7-8; Gen. 6:5; Col. 3:10; Eph. 4:24, <strong>c.</strong> James 1:14-15; Ps. 53:1-3; Matt. 15:19; Rom. 3:10-18, 23; Gal. 5:19-21</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q26"></span>
### Question 26: How is original sin conveyed from our first parents <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> their posterity?
Answer: Original sin is conveyed from our first parents <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> their posterity by natural generation, so <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">as</span> all that proceed from them in that way are conceived and born in sin.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q26-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 26">d</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q26-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>d.</strong> Ps. 51:5; Job 14:4; John 3:6</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q27"></span>
### Question 27: What misery did the fall bring upon mankind?
Answer: The fall brought upon mankind the loss of communion with God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q27-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 27">e</a></sup> his displeasure and curse;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q27-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 27">f</a></sup> so <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">as</span> we are by nature children of wrath,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q27-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 27">g</a></sup> bond slaves to Satan,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q27-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 27">h</a></sup> and justly liable to all punishments in this world, and that which is to come.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q27-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 27">i</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q27-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>e.</strong> Gen. 3:8, 10, 24; John 8:34, 42, 44; Eph. 2:12, <strong>f.</strong> Gen. 3:16-19; Job 5:7; Eccl. 2:22-23; Rom. 8:18-23, <strong>g.</strong> Eph. 2:2-3; John 3:36; Rom. 1:18; Eph. 5:6, <strong>h.</strong> 2 Tim. 2:26, <strong>i.</strong> Gen. 2:17; Lam. 3:39; Rom. 6:23; Matt. 25:41, 46; Jude 7</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q28"></span>
### Question 28: What are the punishments of sin in this world?
Answer: The punishments of sin in this world are either inward, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="such"></span> as blindness of mind,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q28-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 28">k</a></sup> a reprobate sense,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q28-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 28">l</a></sup> strong delusions,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q28-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 28">m</a></sup> hardness of heart,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q28-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 28">n</a></sup> horror of conscience,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q28-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 28">o</a></sup> and vile affections;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q28-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 28">p</a></sup> or outward, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="such"></span> as the curse of God upon the creatures for our sakes,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q28-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 28">q</a></sup> and all other evils that befall us in our bodies, names, estates, relations, and employments;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q28-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 28">r</a></sup> together with death itself.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q28-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 28">s</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q28-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>k.</strong> Eph. 4:18, <strong>l.</strong> Rom. 1:28, <strong>m.</strong> 2 Thess. 2:11, <strong>n.</strong> Rom. 2:5, <strong>o.</strong> Isa. 33:14; Gen. 4:13; Matt. 27:4, <strong>p.</strong> Rom. 1:26, <strong>q.</strong> Gen. 3:17, <strong>r.</strong> Deut. 28:15, <strong>s.</strong> Rom. 6:21, 23</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q29"></span>
### Question 29: What are the punishments of sin in the world to come?
Answer: The punishments of sin in the world to come, are everlasting separation from the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="comforting">comfortable</span> presence of God, and most grievous torments in soul and body, without intermission, in hellfire forever.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q29-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 29">t</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q29-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>t.</strong> 2 Thess. 1:9; Mark 9:43-44, 46, 48; Luke 16:24, 26; Matt. 25:41, 46; Rev. 14:11; John 3:36</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q30"></span>
### Question 30: <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Does">Doth</span> God leave all mankind to perish in the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> of sin and misery?
Answer: God <span class="text-variant" data-modern="does">doth</span> not leave all men to perish in the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> of sin and misery,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q30-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 30">u</a></sup> into which they fell by the breach of the first covenant, commonly called the covenant of works;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q30-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 30">w</a></sup> but of his mere love and mercy <span class="text-variant" data-modern="delivers">delivereth</span> his elect out of it, and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="brings">bringeth</span> them into <span class="text-variant" data-modern="a state">an</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">estate</span> of salvation by the second covenant, commonly called the covenant of grace.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q30-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 30">x</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q30-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>u.</strong> 1 Thess. 5:9, <strong>w.</strong> Gen. 3:17; Rom. 5:12, 15; Gal. 3:10, 12, <strong>x.</strong> Titus 3:4-7; Gal. 3:21; Rom. 3:20-22; 2 Thess. 2:13-14; Acts 13:48; Eph. 1:4-5</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q31"></span>
### Question 31: With whom was the covenant of grace made?
Answer: The covenant of grace was made with Christ as the second Adam, and in him with all the elect as his seed.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q31-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 31">y</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q31-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>y.</strong> Gal. 3:16; Rom. 5:15; Isa. 53:10-11; Isa. 59:20-21</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q32"></span>
### Question 32: How is the grace of God manifested in the second covenant?
Answer: The grace of God is manifested in the second covenant, in that he freely <span class="text-variant" data-modern="provides">provideth</span> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="offers">offereth</span> to sinners a Mediator,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q32-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 32">z</a></sup> and life and salvation by him;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q32-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 32">a</a></sup> and requiring faith as the condition to <span class="text-variant" data-modern="give them an"></span> interest <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">them</span> in him,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q32-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 32">b</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="promises">promiseth</span> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="gives">giveth</span> his Holy Spirit<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q32-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 32">c</a></sup> to all his elect, to work in them that faith,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q32-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 32">d</a></sup> with all other saving graces;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q32-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 32">e</a></sup> and to enable them <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> all holy obedience,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q32-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 32">f</a></sup> as the evidence of the truth of their faith<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q32-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 32">g</a></sup> and thankfulness to God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q32-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 32">h</a></sup> and as the way <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> he <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has">hath</span> appointed them to salvation.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q32-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 32">i</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q32-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>z.</strong> Gen. 3:15; Isa. 42:6; John 3:16; John 6:27; 1 Tim. 2:5, <strong>a.</strong> 1 John 5:11-12, <strong>b.</strong> John 3:16, 36; John 1:12, <strong>c.</strong> Isa. 59:21; Luke 11:13; John 14:16-20; 1 Cor. 12:13; Rom. 8:9, <strong>d.</strong> 2 Cor. 4:13; 1 Cor. 12:3, 9; Eph. 2:8-10; Acts 16:14; 2 Pet. 1:1, <strong>e.</strong> Gal. 5:22-23, <strong>f.</strong> Ezek. 36:27; Eph. 2:10, <strong>g.</strong> James 2:18, 22, <strong>h.</strong> 2 Cor. 5:14-15, <strong>i.</strong> Eph. 2:10; Titus 2:14</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q33"></span>
### Question 33: Was the covenant of grace always administered <span class="text-variant" data-modern="in">after</span> one and the same manner?
Answer: The covenant of grace was not always administered <span class="text-variant" data-modern="in">after</span> the same manner, but the administrations of it under the old testament were different from those under the new.

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q33-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>k.</strong> 2 Cor. 3:6-9; Heb. 8:7-13</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q34"></span>
### Question 34: How was the covenant of grace administered under the old testament?
Answer: The covenant of grace was administered under the old testament, by promises,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q34-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 34">l</a></sup> prophecies,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q34-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 34">m</a></sup> sacrifices,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q34-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 34">n</a></sup> circumcision,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q34-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 34">o</a></sup> the Passover,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q34-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 34">p</a></sup> and other types and ordinances, which <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">did</span> all <span class="text-variant" data-modern="foresignified">foresignify</span> Christ then to come, and were for that time sufficient to build up the elect in faith in the promised Messiah,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q34-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 34">q</a></sup> by whom they then had full remission of sin, and eternal salvation.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q34-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 34">r</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q34-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>l.</strong> Rom. 15:8; Gen. 3:15, <strong>m.</strong> Acts 3:20, 24; Isa. 52:13-53:12, <strong>n.</strong> Heb. 10:1; Lev. 1, <strong>o.</strong> Rom. 4:11; Gen. 17:1-14, <strong>p.</strong> 1 Cor. 5:7; Ex. 12:14, 17, 24, <strong>q.</strong> Heb. 8:1-2; Heb. 11:13, <strong>r.</strong> Gal. 3:7-9, 14</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q35"></span>
### Question 35: How is the covenant of grace administered under the new testament?
Answer: Under the new testament, when Christ the substance was exhibited, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="1"></span> the same covenant of grace was and still is to be administered in the preaching of the Word,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q35-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 35">s</a></sup> and the administration of the sacraments of baptism<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q35-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 35">t</a></sup> and the Lord’s Supper; in which grace and salvation are held forth in more fullness, evidence, and efficacy, to all nations.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q35-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 35">w</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q35-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>s.</strong> Luke 24:47-48; Matt. 28:19-20, <strong>t.</strong> Matt. 28:19-20, <strong>u.</strong> Matt. 26:28; 1 Cor. 11:23-25, <strong>w.</strong> Rom. 1:16; 2 Cor. 3:6-9; Heb. 8:6, 10-11; Matt. 28:19; Eph. 3:1-12</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q36"></span>
### Question 36: Who is the Mediator of the covenant of grace?
Answer: The only Mediator of the covenant of grace is the Lord Jesus Christ,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q36-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 36">x</a></sup> who, being the eternal Son of God, of one substance and equal with the Father,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q36-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 36">y</a></sup> in the fullness of time became man,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q36-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 36">z</a></sup> and so was and continues to be God and man, in two <span class="text-variant" data-modern="entire,">entire</span> distinct natures, and one person, forever.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q36-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 36">a</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q36-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>x.</strong> 1 Tim. 2:5; John 14:6; Acts 4:12, <strong>y.</strong> John 1:1, 14, 18; John 10:30; Phil. 2:6; Ps. 2:7; Matt. 3:17; Matt. 17:5, <strong>z.</strong> Gal. 4:4; Matt. 1:23; John 1:14, <strong>a.</strong> Luke 1:35; Acts 1:11; Rom. 9:5; Col. 2:9; Heb. 7:24-25; Heb. 13:8; Phil. 2:5-11</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q37"></span>
### Question 37: How did Christ, being the Son of God, become man?
Answer: Christ the Son of God became man, by taking to himself a true body, and a reasonable soul,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q37-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 37">b</a></sup> being conceived by the power of the Holy <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Spirit">Ghost</span> in the womb of the virgin Mary, of her substance, and born of her,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q37-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 37">c</a></sup> yet without sin.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q37-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 37">d</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern=".">1In</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">the</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">new</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">testament,</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">Christ</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">is</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">set</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">forth,</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">who</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">is</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">the</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">substance</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">of</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">the</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">covenant</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">of</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">grace,</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">which</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">is</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">more</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">fully</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">dispensed</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">through</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">the</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">Word</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">and</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">sacraments</span>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q37-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>b.</strong> John 1:14; Matt. 26:38; Phil. 2:7; Heb. 2:14-17; Luke 2:40, 52; John 11:33, <strong>c.</strong> Luke 1:27, 31, 35; Gal. 4:4, <strong>d.</strong> Heb. 4:15; Heb. 7:26; 2 Cor. 5:21; 1 John 3:5</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q38"></span>
### Question 38: Why was it requisite that the Mediator should be God?
Answer: It was requisite that the Mediator should be God, that he might sustain and keep the human nature from sinking under the infinite wrath of God, and the power of death;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q38-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 38">e</a></sup> give worth and efficacy to his sufferings, obedience, and intercession;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q38-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 38">f</a></sup> and to satisfy God’s justice,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q38-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 38">g</a></sup> procure his favor,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q38-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 38">h</a></sup> purchase a <span class="text-variant" data-modern="special">peculiar</span> people,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q38-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 38">i</a></sup> give his Spirit to them,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q38-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 38">k</a></sup> conquer all their enemies,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q38-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 38">l</a></sup> and bring them to everlasting salvation.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q38-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 38">m</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q38-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>e.</strong> Acts 2:24-25; Rom. 1:4; Rom. 4:25; Heb. 9:14, <strong>f.</strong> Acts 20:28; Heb. 9:14; Heb. 7:25-28; John 17, <strong>g.</strong> Rom. 3:24-26, <strong>h.</strong> Eph. 1:6; Matt. 3:17, <strong>i.</strong> Titus 2:13-14, <strong>k.</strong> Gal. 4:6; John 15:26; John 16:7, <strong>l.</strong> Luke 1:68-69, 71, 74, <strong>m.</strong> Heb. 5:8-9; Heb. 9:11-15</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q39"></span>
### Question 39: Why was it requisite that the Mediator should be man?
Answer: It was requisite that the Mediator should be man, that he might advance our nature,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q39-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 39">n</a></sup> perform obedience to the law,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q39-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 39">o</a></sup> suffer and make intercession for us in our nature,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q39-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 39">p</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="sympathize with">have</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">a</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">fellow</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">feeling</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">of</span> our <span class="text-variant" data-modern="weaknesses;">infirmities;</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q39-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 39">q</a></sup> that we might receive the adoption of sons,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q39-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 39">r</a></sup> and have comfort and access with boldness <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> the throne of grace.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q39-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 39">s</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q39-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>n.</strong> Heb. 2:16; 2 Pet. 1:4, <strong>o.</strong> Gal. 4:4; Matt. 5:17; Rom. 5:19; Phil. 2:8, <strong>p.</strong> Heb. 2:14; Heb. 7:24-25, <strong>q.</strong> Heb. 4:15, <strong>r.</strong> Gal. 4:5, <strong>s.</strong> Heb. 4:16</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q40"></span>
### Question 40: Why was it requisite that the Mediator should be God and man in one person?
Answer: It was requisite that the Mediator, who was to reconcile God and man, should himself be both God and man, and this in one person, that the proper works of each nature might be accepted <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by">of</span> God for us,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q40-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 40">t</a></sup> and relied on by us, as the works of the whole person.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q40-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 40">u</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q40-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>t.</strong> Matt. 1:21, 23; Matt. 3:17; Heb. 9:14, <strong>u.</strong> 1 Pet. 2:6</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q41"></span>
### Question 41: Why was our Mediator called Jesus?
Answer: Our Mediator was called Jesus, because he <span class="text-variant" data-modern="saves">saveth</span> his people from their sins.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q41-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 41">w</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q41-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>w.</strong> Matt. 1:21</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q42"></span>
### Question 42: Why was our Mediator called Christ?
Answer: Our Mediator was called Christ, because he was anointed with the Holy <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Spirit">Ghost</span> above measure;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q42-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 42">x</a></sup> and so set apart, and fully furnished with all authority and ability,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q42-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 42">y</a></sup> to execute the offices of prophet,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q42-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 42">z</a></sup> priest,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q42-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 42">a</a></sup> and king of his church,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q42-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 42">b</a></sup> in the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> both of his humiliation and exaltation.

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q42-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>x.</strong> Matt. 3:16; Acts 10:37-38; John 3:34; Ps. 45:7, <strong>y.</strong> John 6:27; Matt. 28:18-20; Rom. 1:3-4, <strong>z.</strong> Acts 3:21-22; Luke 4:18, 21; Heb. 1:1-2; Deut. 18:18, <strong>a.</strong> Heb. 5:5-7; Heb. 4:14-15, <strong>b.</strong> Ps. 2:6; Luke 1:32-34; John 18:37; Matt. 21:5; Isa. 9:6-7; Phil. 2:8-11</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q43"></span>
### Question 43: How <span class="text-variant" data-modern="does">doth</span> Christ execute the office of a prophet?
Answer: Christ <span class="text-variant" data-modern="executes">executeth</span> the office of a prophet, in his revealing to the church,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q43-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 43">c</a></sup> in all ages, by his Spirit and Word,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q43-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 43">d</a></sup> in <span class="text-variant" data-modern="various">divers</span> ways of administration,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q43-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 43">e</a></sup> the whole will of God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q43-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 43">f</a></sup> in all things concerning their edification and salvation.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q43-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 43">g</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q43-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>c.</strong> John 1:18, <strong>d.</strong> 1 Pet. 1:10-12, <strong>e.</strong> Heb. 1:1-2, <strong>f.</strong> John 15:15, <strong>g.</strong> Acts 20:32; Eph. 4:11-13; John 20:31</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q44"></span>
### Question 44: How <span class="text-variant" data-modern="does">doth</span> Christ execute the office of a priest?
Answer: Christ <span class="text-variant" data-modern="executes">executeth</span> the office of a priest, in his once offering himself <span class="text-variant" data-modern="as"></span> a sacrifice without spot to God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q44-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 44">h</a></sup> to be a reconciliation for the sins of his people;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q44-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 44">i</a></sup> and in making continual intercession for them.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q44-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 44">k</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q44-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>h.</strong> Heb. 9:14, 28; Heb. 10:12; Isa. 53, <strong>i.</strong> Heb. 2:17; 2 Cor. 5:18; Col. 1:21-22, <strong>k.</strong> Heb. 7:25; Heb. 9:24</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q45"></span>
### Question 45: How <span class="text-variant" data-modern="does">doth</span> Christ execute the office of a king?
Answer: Christ <span class="text-variant" data-modern="executes">executeth</span> the office of a king, in calling out of the world a people to himself,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q45-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 45">l</a></sup> and giving them officers,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q45-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 45">m</a></sup> laws,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q45-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 45">n</a></sup> and censures, by which he visibly governs them;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q45-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 45">o</a></sup> in bestowing saving grace upon his elect,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q45-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 45">p</a></sup> rewarding their obedience,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q45-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 45">q</a></sup> and correcting them for their sins,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q45-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 45">r</a></sup> preserving and supporting them under all their temptations and sufferings,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q45-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 45">s</a></sup> restraining and overcoming all their enemies,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q45-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 45">t</a></sup> and powerfully ordering all things for his own glory,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q45-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 45">u</a></sup> and their good;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q45-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 45">w</a></sup> and also in taking vengeance on the rest, who <span class="text-variant" data-modern="do not"></span> know <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">not</span> God, and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="do not"></span> obey <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">not</span> the gospel.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q45-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 45">x</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q45-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>l.</strong> Acts 15:14-16; Gen. 49:10; Ps. 110:3; John 17:2, <strong>m.</strong> Eph. 4:11-12; 1 Cor. 12:28, <strong>n.</strong> Isa. 33:22, <strong>o.</strong> Matt. 18:17-18, <strong>p.</strong> Acts 5:31, <strong>q.</strong> Rev. 22:12; Rev. 2:10, <strong>r.</strong> Rev. 3:19, <strong>s.</strong> Isa. 63:9, <strong>t.</strong> 1 Cor. 15:25; Ps. 110:1-2, <strong>u.</strong> Rom. 14:10-11, <strong>w.</strong> Rom. 8:28, <strong>x.</strong> 2 Thess. 1:8-9; Ps. 2:8-9</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q46"></span>
### Question 46: What was the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> of Christ’s humiliation?
Answer: The <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> of Christ’s humiliation was that low condition, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="in which">wherein</span> he for our sakes, emptying himself of his glory, took upon <span class="text-variant" data-modern="himself">him</span> the form of a servant,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q46-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 46">y</a></sup> in his conception<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q46-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 46">z</a></sup> and birth,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q46-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 46">a</a></sup> life,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q46-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 46">b</a></sup> death,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q46-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 46">c</a></sup> and after his death,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q46-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 46">d</a></sup> until his resurrection.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q46-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 46">e</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q46-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>y.</strong> Phil. 2:6-8, <strong>z.</strong> Luke 1:31, <strong>a.</strong> Luke 2:7, <strong>b.</strong> Gal. 4:4; 2 Cor. 8:9; Luke 9:58; Heb. 2:18; Isa. 53:3, <strong>c.</strong> Ps. 22:1; Matt. 27:46; Isa. 53:10; 1 John 2:2; Phil. 2:8, <strong>d.</strong> Matt. 12:40; 1 Cor. 15:3-4, <strong>e.</strong> Acts 2:24-27, 31</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q47"></span>
### Question 47: How did Christ humble himself in his conception and birth?
Answer: Christ humbled himself in his conception and birth, in that, being from all eternity the Son of God, in the bosom of the Father, he was pleased in the fullness of time to become the son of man, made of a woman of low <span class="text-variant" data-modern="condition,">estate,</span> and to be born of her; with <span class="text-variant" data-modern="various">divers</span> circumstances of more than ordinary abasement.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q47-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 47">f</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q47-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>f.</strong> John 1:14, 18; Gal. 4:4; Luke 2:7</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q48"></span>
### Question 48: How did Christ humble himself in his life?
Answer: Christ humbled himself in his life, by subjecting himself to the law,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q48-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 48">g</a></sup> which he perfectly fulfilled;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q48-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 48">h</a></sup> and by conflicting with the indignities of the world,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q48-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 48">i</a></sup> temptations of Satan,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q48-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 48">k</a></sup> and infirmities in his flesh, whether common to the nature of man, or particularly accompanying <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">that</span> his low condition.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q48-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 48">l</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q48-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>g.</strong> Gal. 4:4, <strong>h.</strong> Matt. 5:17; Rom. 5:19, <strong>i.</strong> Ps. 22:6; Isa. 53:2-3; Heb. 12:2-3, <strong>k.</strong> Matt. 4:1-11; Luke 4:13, <strong>l.</strong> Heb. 2:17-18; Heb. 4:15; Isa. 52:13-14</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q49"></span>
### Question 49: How did Christ humble himself in his death?
Answer: Christ humbled himself in his death, in that having been betrayed by Judas,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q49-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 49">m</a></sup> forsaken by his disciples,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q49-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 49">n</a></sup> scorned and rejected by the world,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q49-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 49">o</a></sup> condemned by Pilate, and tormented by his persecutors;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q49-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 49">p</a></sup> having also conflicted with the terrors of death, and the powers of darkness, felt and borne the weight of God’s wrath,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q49-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 49">q</a></sup> he laid down his life <span class="text-variant" data-modern="as"></span> an offering for sin,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q49-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 49">r</a></sup> enduring the painful, shameful, and cursed death of the cross.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q49-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 49">s</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q49-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>m.</strong> Matt. 27:4, <strong>n.</strong> Matt. 26:56, <strong>o.</strong> Isa. 53:2-3, <strong>p.</strong> Matt. 27:26-50; John 19:34; Luke 22:63-64, <strong>q.</strong> Luke 22:44; Matt. 27:46, <strong>r.</strong> Isa. 53:10; Matt. 20:28; Mark 10:45, <strong>s.</strong> Phil. 2:8; Heb. 12:2; Gal. 3:13</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q50"></span>
### Question 50: <span class="text-variant" data-modern="What was">Wherein</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">consisted</span> Christ’s humiliation after his death?
Answer: Christ’s humiliation after his death consisted in his being buried,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q50-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 50">t</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">and</span> continuing in the state of the dead, and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="continuing"></span> under the power of death till the third day;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q50-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 50">u</a></sup> which <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has">hath</span> been otherwise expressed in these words, He descended into hell.

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q50-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>t.</strong> 1 Cor. 15:3-4, <strong>u.</strong> Ps. 16:10; Acts 2:24-27, 31; Rom. 6:9; Matt. 12:40</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q51"></span>
### Question 51: What was the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> of Christ’s exaltation?
Answer: The <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> of Christ’s exaltation <span class="text-variant" data-modern="comprehends">comprehendeth</span> his resurrection,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q51-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 51">w</a></sup> ascension,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q51-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 51">x</a></sup> sitting at the right hand of the Father,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q51-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 51">y</a></sup> and his coming again to judge the world.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q51-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 51">z</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q51-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>w.</strong> 1 Cor. 15:4, <strong>x.</strong> Ps. 68:18; Acts 1:11; Eph. 4:8, <strong>y.</strong> Eph. 1:20; Ps. 110:1; Acts 2:33-34; Heb. 1:3, <strong>z.</strong> Acts 1:11; Acts 17:31; Matt. 16:27</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q52"></span>
### Question 52: How was Christ exalted in his resurrection?
Answer: Christ was exalted in his resurrection, in that, not having seen corruption in <span class="text-variant" data-modern="death (by">death,</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">(of</span> which it was not possible for him to be held,) and having the very same body in which he suffered, with the essential properties <span class="text-variant" data-modern="of it">thereof,</span> (but without mortality, and other common infirmities belonging to this life,) really united to his soul,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q52-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 52">c</a></sup> he rose again from the dead the third day by his own power;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q52-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 52">d</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by which">whereby</span> he declared himself to be the Son of God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q52-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 52">e</a></sup> to have satisfied divine justice,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q52-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 52">f</a></sup> to have vanquished death, and him that had the power of it,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q52-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 52">g</a></sup> and to be Lord of <span class="text-variant" data-modern="the living">quick</span> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="the"></span> dead:<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q52-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 52">h</a></sup> all <span class="text-variant" data-modern="of"></span> which he did as a public person,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q52-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 52">i</a></sup> the head of his church,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q52-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 52">k</a></sup> for their justification,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q52-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 52">l</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="making alive">quickening</span> in grace,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q52-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 52">m</a></sup> support against enemies,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q52-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 52">n</a></sup> and to assure them of their resurrection from the dead at the last day.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q52-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 52">o</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q52-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>a.</strong> Acts 2:24, 27, <strong>b.</strong> Luke 24:39, <strong>c.</strong> Rom. 6:9; Rev. 1:18, <strong>d.</strong> John 10:18, <strong>e.</strong> Rom. 1:4, <strong>f.</strong> Rom. 8:34; Rom. 3:25-26; Heb. 9:13-14, <strong>g.</strong> Heb. 2:14, <strong>h.</strong> Rom. 14:9, <strong>i.</strong> 1 Cor. 15:21-22; Isa. 53:10-11, <strong>k.</strong> Eph. 1:20-23; Col. 1:18, <strong>l.</strong> Rom. 4:25, <strong>m.</strong> Eph. 2:1, 5-6; Col. 2:12, <strong>n.</strong> 1 Cor. 15:25-27; Ps. 2:7-9, <strong>o.</strong> 1 Cor. 15:20; 1 Thess. 4:14</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q53"></span>
### Question 53: How was Christ exalted in his ascension?
Answer: Christ was exalted in his ascension, in that having after his resurrection often appeared <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> and conversed with his apostles, speaking to them of the things pertaining to the kingdom of God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q53-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 53">p</a></sup> and giving them commission to preach the gospel to all nations,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q53-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 53">q</a></sup> forty days after his resurrection, he, in our nature, and as our head,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q53-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 53">r</a></sup> triumphing over enemies,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q53-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 53">s</a></sup> visibly went up into the highest heavens, there to receive gifts for men,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q53-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 53">t</a></sup> to raise up our affections <span class="text-variant" data-modern="heavenward,">thither,</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q53-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 53">u</a></sup> and to prepare a place for us,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q53-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 53">w</a></sup> where <span class="text-variant" data-modern="he"></span> himself is, and shall continue till his second coming at the end of the world.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q53-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 53">x</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q53-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>p.</strong> Acts 1:2-3, <strong>q.</strong> Matt. 28:19-20, <strong>r.</strong> John 20:17; Heb. 6:20, <strong>s.</strong> Eph. 4:8, <strong>t.</strong> Acts 1:9-11; Eph. 4:7-8; Ps. 68:18; Eph. 4:10; Acts 2:33, <strong>u.</strong> Col. 3:1-2, <strong>w.</strong> John 14:3, <strong>x.</strong> Acts 3:21</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q54"></span>
### Question 54: How is Christ exalted in his sitting at the right hand of God?
Answer: Christ is exalted in his sitting at the right hand of God, in that as God-man he is advanced to the highest favor with God the Father,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q54-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 54">y</a></sup> with all fullness of joy,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q54-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 54">z</a></sup> glory,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q54-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 54">a</a></sup> and power over all things in heaven and earth;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q54-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 54">b</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="gathers"></span> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="defends">doth</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">gather</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">and</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">defend</span> his church, and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="subdues">subdue</span> their enemies; <span class="text-variant" data-modern="and furnishes">furnisheth</span> his ministers and people with gifts and graces,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q54-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 54">c</a></sup> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="makes">maketh</span> intercession for them.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q54-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 54">d</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q54-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>y.</strong> Phil. 2:9, <strong>z.</strong> Acts 2:28; Ps. 16:11, <strong>a.</strong> John 17:5, <strong>b.</strong> Dan. 7:13-14; Eph. 1:22; 1 Pet. 3:22, <strong>c.</strong> Eph. 4:10-12; Ps. 110:1; Heb. 10:12-14; Ezek. 37:24, <strong>d.</strong> Rom. 8:34; 1 John 2:1; Heb. 7:25</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q55"></span>
### Question 55: How <span class="text-variant" data-modern="does">doth</span> Christ make intercession?
Answer: Christ <span class="text-variant" data-modern="makes">maketh</span> intercession, by his appearing in our nature continually before the Father in heaven,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q55-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 55">e</a></sup> in the merit of his obedience and sacrifice on earth,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q55-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 55">f</a></sup> declaring his will to have it applied to all believers;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q55-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 55">g</a></sup> answering all accusations against them,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q55-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 55">h</a></sup> and procuring for them quiet of conscience, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="in spite of">notwithstanding</span> daily failings,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q55-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 55">i</a></sup> access with boldness to the throne of grace,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q55-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 55">k</a></sup> and acceptance of their persons<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q55-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 55">l</a></sup> and services.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q55-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 55">m</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q55-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>e.</strong> Heb. 9:12, 24, <strong>f.</strong> Isa. 53:12; Heb. 1:3, <strong>g.</strong> John 3:16; John 17:9, 20, 24, <strong>h.</strong> Rom. 8:33-34, <strong>i.</strong> Rom. 5:1-2; 1 John 2:1-2, <strong>k.</strong> Heb. 4:16, <strong>l.</strong> Eph. 1:6, <strong>m.</strong> 1 Pet. 2:5</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q56"></span>
### Question 56: How is Christ to be exalted in his coming again to judge the world?
Answer: Christ is to be exalted in his coming again to judge the world, in that he, who was unjustly judged and condemned by wicked men,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q56-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 56">n</a></sup> shall come again at the last day in great power,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q56-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 56">o</a></sup> and in the full manifestation of his own glory, and of his Father’s, with all his holy angels,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q56-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 56">p</a></sup> with a shout, with the voice of the archangel, and with the trumpet of God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q56-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 56">q</a></sup> to judge the world in righteousness.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q56-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 56">r</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q56-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>n.</strong> Acts 3:14-15, <strong>o.</strong> Matt. 24:30; 2 Thess. 1:9-10, <strong>p.</strong> Luke 9:26; Matt. 25:31, <strong>q.</strong> 1 Thess. 4:16, <strong>r.</strong> Acts 17:31; 2 Thess. 1:6-8</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q57"></span>
### Question 57: What benefits <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has">hath</span> Christ procured by his mediation?
