Answer: Christ, by his mediation, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has">hath</span> procured redemption,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q57-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 57">s</a></sup> with all other benefits of the covenant of grace.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q57-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 57">t</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q57-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>s.</strong> 1 Tim. 2:5-6; Heb. 9:12; Eph. 1:7, <strong>t.</strong> 2 Cor. 1:20; Eph. 1:3-6; 2 Pet. 1:3-4</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q58"></span>
### Question 58: How <span class="text-variant" data-modern="are">do</span> we <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">come</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">to</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">be</span> made partakers of the benefits <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> Christ <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has">hath</span> procured?
Answer: We are made partakers of the benefits <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> Christ <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has">hath</span> procured, by the application of them <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> us,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q58-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 58">u</a></sup> which is the work especially of God the Holy <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Spirit.">Ghost.</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q58-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 58">w</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q58-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>u.</strong> John 1:11-12, <strong>w.</strong> Titus 3:4-7; John 16:14-15; John 3:3-8</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q59"></span>
### Question 59: Who are made partakers of redemption through Christ?
Answer: Redemption is certainly applied, and effectually communicated, to all those for whom Christ <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has">hath</span> purchased it;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q59-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 59">x</a></sup> who are in time by the Holy <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Spirit">Ghost</span> enabled to believe in Christ according to the gospel.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q59-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 59">y</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q59-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>x.</strong> Eph. 1:13-14; John 6:37, 39; John 10:15-16, <strong>y.</strong> Rom. 10:17; 1 Cor. 2:12-16; Eph. 2:8; Rom. 8:9, 14</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q60"></span>
### Question 60: Can <span class="text-variant" data-modern="those">they</span> who have never heard the gospel, and so <span class="text-variant" data-modern="do not"></span> know <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">not</span> Jesus Christ, nor believe in him, be saved by their living according to the light of nature?
Answer: <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Those">They</span> who, having never heard the gospel,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q60-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 60">z</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="do not"></span> know <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">not</span> Jesus Christ,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q60-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 60">a</a></sup> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="do not"></span> believe <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">not</span> in him, cannot be saved,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q60-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 60">b</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="despite their utmost diligence">be</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">they</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">never</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">so</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">diligent</span> to frame their lives according to the light of nature,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q60-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 60">c</a></sup> or the laws of that religion which they profess;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q60-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 60">d</a></sup> neither is there salvation in any other, but in Christ alone,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q60-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 60">e</a></sup> who is the Savior only of his body the church.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q60-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 60">f</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q60-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>z.</strong> Rom. 10:14, <strong>a.</strong> 2 Thess. 1:8-9; Eph. 2:12; John 1:10-12, <strong>b.</strong> John 8:24; John 3:18, <strong>c.</strong> 1 Cor. 1:20-24, <strong>d.</strong> John 4:22; Rom. 9:31-32; Phil. 3:4-9, <strong>e.</strong> Acts 4:12, <strong>f.</strong> Eph. 5:23</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q61"></span>
### Question 61: Are all <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">they</span> saved <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">who</span> hear the gospel, and live in the church?
Answer: All that hear the gospel, and live in the visible church, are not saved; but <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">they</span> only <span class="text-variant" data-modern="those"></span> who are true members of the church invisible.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q61-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 61">g</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q61-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>g.</strong> John 12:38-40; Rom. 9:6; Matt. 22:14; Matt. 7:21; Rom. 11:7; 1 Cor. 10:2-5</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q62"></span>
### Question 62: What is the visible church?
Answer: The visible church is a society made up of all <span class="text-variant" data-modern="those who">such</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">as</span> in all ages and places of the world <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">do</span> profess the true religion,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q62-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 62">h</a></sup> and of their children.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q62-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 62">i</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q62-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>h.</strong> 1 Cor. 1:2; 1 Cor. 12:13; Rom. 15:9-12; Rev. 7:9; Ps. 2:8; Ps. 22:27-31; Ps. 45:17; Matt. 28:19-20; Isa. 59:21, <strong>i.</strong> 1 Cor. 7:14; Acts 2:39; Rom. 11:16; Gen. 17:7</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q63"></span>
### Question 63: What are the special privileges of the visible church?
Answer: The visible church <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has">hath</span> the privilege of being under God’s special care and government;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q63-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 63">k</a></sup> of being protected and preserved in all ages, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="in spite of">notwithstanding</span> the opposition of all enemies;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q63-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 63">l</a></sup> and of enjoying the communion of saints, the ordinary means of salvation,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q63-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 63">m</a></sup> and offers of grace by Christ to all the members of it in the ministry of the gospel, testifying, that <span class="text-variant" data-modern="whoever">whosoever</span> believes in him shall be saved,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q63-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 63">n</a></sup> and excluding none that will come <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> him.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q63-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 63">o</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q63-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>k.</strong> Isa. 4:5-6; 1 Tim. 4:10; Eph. 4:11-13, <strong>l.</strong> Ps. 115:1-2, 9; Isa. 31:4-5; Zech. 12:2-4, 8-9; Matt. 16:18, <strong>m.</strong> Acts 2:39, 42; Matt. 28:19-20; 1 Cor. 12:12-13, <strong>n.</strong> Ps. 147:19-20; Rom. 9:4; Eph. 4:11-12; Acts 22:16; Acts 2:21; Joel 2:32; Rom. 10:10-13, 17, <strong>o.</strong> Matt. 11:28-29; John 6:37</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q64"></span>
### Question 64: What is the invisible church?
Answer: The invisible church is the whole number of the elect, that have been, are, or shall be gathered into one under Christ the head.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q64-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 64">p</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q64-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>p.</strong> Eph. 1:10; Eph. 1:22-23; John 10:16; John 11:52; Eph. 5:23, 27, 32</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q65"></span>
### Question 65: What special benefits do the members of the invisible church enjoy by Christ?
Answer: The members of the invisible church by Christ enjoy union and communion with him in grace and glory.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q65-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 65">q</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q65-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>q.</strong> John 17:21; Eph. 2:5-6; John 17:24; 1 John 1:3; John 1:16; Eph. 3:16-19; Phil. 3:10; Rom. 6:5-6</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q66"></span>
### Question 66: What is <span class="text-variant" data-modern="the union"></span> that <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">union</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">which</span> the elect have with Christ?
Answer: The union <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> the elect have with Christ is the work of God’s grace,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q66-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 66">r</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by which">whereby</span> they are spiritually and mystically, yet really and inseparably, joined to Christ as their head and husband;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q66-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 66">s</a></sup> which is done in their effectual calling.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q66-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 66">t</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q66-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>r.</strong> Eph. 2:6-7, <strong>s.</strong> Eph. 1:22; 1 Cor. 6:17; John 10:28; Eph. 5:23, 30; John 15:5; Eph. 3:17, <strong>t.</strong> 1 Pet. 5:10; 1 Cor. 1:9</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q67"></span>
### Question 67: What is effectual calling?
Answer: Effectual calling is the work of God’s almighty power and grace,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q67-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 67">u</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by which">whereby</span> (out of his free and special love to his elect, and from nothing in them moving him <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to it)">thereunto)</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">he</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">doth,</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q67-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 67">w</a></sup> in his accepted time, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="he invites">invite</span> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="draws">draw</span> them to Jesus Christ, by his Word and Spirit;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q67-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 67">x</a></sup> savingly enlightening their minds,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q67-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 67">y</a></sup> renewing and powerfully determining their wills,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q67-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 67">z</a></sup> so <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">as</span> they (although in themselves dead in sin) are hereby made willing and able freely to answer his call, and to accept and embrace the grace offered and conveyed <span class="text-variant" data-modern="in it.">therein.</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q67-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 67">a</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q67-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>u.</strong> Ezek. 37:9, 14; John 5:25; Eph. 1:18-20; 2 Tim. 1:8-9, <strong>w.</strong> Titus 3:4-5; Eph. 2:4-5, 7-9; Rom. 9:11; Deut. 9:5, <strong>x.</strong> John 3:5; Titus 3:5; 2 Cor. 5:20; 2 Cor. 6:1-2; John 6:44-45; Acts 16:14; 2 Thess. 2:13-14, <strong>y.</strong> Acts 26:18; 1 Cor. 2:10, 12; 2 Cor. 4:6; Eph. 1:17-18, <strong>z.</strong> Ezek. 11:19; Ezek. 36:26-27; John 6:45, <strong>a.</strong> Eph. 2:5; Phil. 2:13; Deut. 30:6; Isa. 45:22; Matt. 11:28-30; Rev. 22:17</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q68"></span>
### Question 68: Are <span class="text-variant" data-modern="only"></span> the elect <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">only</span> effectually called?
Answer: All the elect, and they only, are effectually called;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q68-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 68">b</a></sup> although others may be, and often are, outwardly called by the ministry of the Word,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q68-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 68">c</a></sup> and have some common operations of the Spirit;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q68-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 68">d</a></sup> who, for their willful neglect and contempt of the grace offered to them, being justly left in their unbelief, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">do</span> never truly come to Jesus Christ.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q68-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 68">e</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q68-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>b.</strong> Acts 13:48, <strong>c.</strong> Matt. 22:14; Acts 8:13, 20-21, <strong>d.</strong> Matt. 7:22; Matt. 13:20-21; Heb. 6:4-6, <strong>e.</strong> John 12:38-40; Acts 28:25-27; John 6:64-65; Ps. 81:11-12; Heb. 10:29; 1 John 2:19</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q69"></span>
### Question 69: What is the communion in grace <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> the members of the invisible church have with Christ?
Answer: The communion in grace <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> the members of the invisible church have with Christ, is their partaking of the virtue of his mediation, in their justification,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q69-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 69">f</a></sup> adoption,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q69-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 69">g</a></sup> sanctification, and whatever else, in this life, manifests their union with him.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q69-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 69">h</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q69-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>f.</strong> Rom. 8:30, <strong>g.</strong> Eph. 1:5, <strong>h.</strong> 1 Cor. 1:30; 1 Cor. 6:11</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q70"></span>
### Question 70: What is justification?
Answer: Justification is an act of God’s free grace <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> sinners,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q70-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 70">i</a></sup> in which he <span class="text-variant" data-modern="pardons">pardoneth</span> all their sins, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="accepts">accepteth</span> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="accounts">accounteth</span> their persons righteous in his sight;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q70-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 70">k</a></sup> not for anything wrought in them, or done by them,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q70-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 70">l</a></sup> but only for the perfect obedience and full satisfaction of Christ, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">by</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">God</span> imputed to <span class="text-variant" data-modern="them by God,">them,</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q70-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 70">m</a></sup> and received by faith alone.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q70-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 70">n</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q70-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>i.</strong> Rom. 3:22, 24-25; Rom. 4:5, <strong>k.</strong> Jer. 23:6; Rom. 4:6-8; 2 Cor. 5:19, 21; Rom. 3:22, 24-25, 27-28, <strong>l.</strong> Titus 3:5, 7; Eph. 1:7, <strong>m.</strong> Rom. 4:6-8, 11; Rom. 5:17-19, <strong>n.</strong> Acts 10:43; Gal. 2:16; Phil. 3:9</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q71"></span>
### Question 71: How is justification an act of God’s free grace?
Answer: Although Christ, by his obedience and death, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="made">did</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">make</span> a proper, real, and full satisfaction to God’s justice <span class="text-variant" data-modern="on">in</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">the</span> behalf of <span class="text-variant" data-modern="those who">them</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">that</span> are justified;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q71-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 71">o</a></sup> yet <span class="text-variant" data-modern="because">inasmuch</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">as</span> God <span class="text-variant" data-modern="accepts">accepteth</span> the satisfaction from a surety, which he might have demanded of them, and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="provided">did</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">provide</span> this surety, his own only Son,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q71-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 71">p</a></sup> imputing his righteousness to them,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q71-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 71">q</a></sup> and requiring nothing of them for their justification but faith,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q71-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 71">r</a></sup> which also is his gift,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q71-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 71">s</a></sup> their justification is to them of free grace.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q71-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 71">t</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q71-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>o.</strong> Rom. 5:8-10, 19, <strong>p.</strong> 1 Tim. 2:5-6; Heb. 10:10; Matt. 20:28; Dan. 9:24, 26; Isa. 53:4-6, 10-12; Heb. 7:22; Rom. 8:32; 1 Pet. 1:18-19, <strong>q.</strong> 2 Cor. 5:21; Rom. 4:6, 11, <strong>r.</strong> Rom. 3:24-25, <strong>s.</strong> Eph. 2:8, <strong>t.</strong> Eph. 1:7; Rom. 3:24-25</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q72"></span>
### Question 72: What is justifying faith?
Answer: Justifying faith is a saving grace,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q72-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 72">u</a></sup> wrought in the heart of a sinner by the Spirit<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q72-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 72">w</a></sup> and Word of God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q72-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 72">x</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by which">whereby</span> he, being convinced of his sin and misery, and of the disability in himself and all other creatures to recover him out of his lost condition,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q72-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 72">y</a></sup> not only <span class="text-variant" data-modern="assents">assenteth</span> to the truth of the promise of the gospel,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q72-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 72">z</a></sup> but <span class="text-variant" data-modern="receives">receiveth</span> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="rests">resteth</span> upon Christ and his righteousness, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">therein</span> held <span class="text-variant" data-modern="forth in it,">forth,</span> for pardon of sin,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q72-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 72">a</a></sup> and for the accepting and accounting of his person righteous in the sight of God for salvation.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q72-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 72">b</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q72-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>u.</strong> Heb. 10:39, <strong>w.</strong> 2 Cor. 4:13; Eph. 1:17-19; 1 Cor. 12:3; 1 Pet. 1:2, <strong>x.</strong> Rom. 10:14-17; 1 Cor. 1:21, <strong>y.</strong> Acts 2:37; Acts 16:30; John 16:8-9; Rom. 6:6; Eph. 2:1; Acts 4:12, <strong>z.</strong> Eph. 1:13; Heb. 11:13, <strong>a.</strong> John 1:12; Acts 16:31; Acts 10:43; Zech. 3:8-9, <strong>b.</strong> Phil. 3:9; Acts 15:11</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q73"></span>
### Question 73: How <span class="text-variant" data-modern="does">doth</span> faith justify a sinner in the sight of God?
Answer: Faith justifies a sinner in the sight of God, not because of those other graces <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">do</span> always accompany it, or of good works that are the fruits of it,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q73-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 73">c</a></sup> nor as if the grace of faith, or any act <span class="text-variant" data-modern="of it,">thereof,</span> were imputed to him for his justification;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q73-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 73">d</a></sup> but only as it is an instrument by which he <span class="text-variant" data-modern="receives">receiveth</span> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="applies">applieth</span> Christ and his righteousness.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q73-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 73">e</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q73-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>c.</strong> Gal. 3:11; Rom. 3:28, <strong>d.</strong> Rom. 4:5; Rom. 10:10, <strong>e.</strong> John 1:12; Phil. 3:9; Gal. 2:16</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q74"></span>
### Question 74: What is adoption?
Answer: Adoption is an act of the free grace of God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q74-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 74">f</a></sup> in and for his only Son Jesus Christ,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q74-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 74">g</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by which">whereby</span> all those that are justified are received into the number of his children,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q74-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 74">h</a></sup> have his name put upon them,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q74-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 74">i</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="have"></span> the Spirit of his Son given to them,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q74-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 74">k</a></sup> are under his fatherly care and dispensations,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q74-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 74">l</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="are"></span> admitted to all the liberties and privileges of the sons of God, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="and are"></span> made heirs of all the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="promises">promises,</span> and fellow heirs with Christ in glory.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q74-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 74">m</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q74-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>f.</strong> 1 John 3:1, <strong>g.</strong> Eph. 1:5; Gal. 4:4-5, <strong>h.</strong> John 1:12; Rom. 8:15-16, <strong>i.</strong> Num. 6:24-27; Amos 9:12; 2 Cor. 6:18; Rev. 3:12, <strong>k.</strong> Gal. 4:6, <strong>l.</strong> Ps. 103:13; Prov. 14:26; Matt. 6:32; Heb. 12:5-7, 11, <strong>m.</strong> Heb. 6:12; Rom. 8:17; 1 Pet. 1:3-4</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q75"></span>
### Question 75: What is sanctification?
Answer: Sanctification is a work of God’s grace, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by which those">whereby</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">they</span> whom God <span class="text-variant" data-modern="has,">hath,</span> before the foundation of the world, chosen to be holy, are in time, through the powerful operation of his Spirit<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q75-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 75">n</a></sup> applying the death and resurrection of Christ <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> them,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q75-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 75">o</a></sup> renewed in their whole man after the image of God;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q75-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 75">p</a></sup> having the seeds of repentance unto life, and all other saving graces, put into their hearts,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q75-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 75">q</a></sup> and those graces so stirred up, increased, and strengthened,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q75-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 75">r</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">as</span> that they more and more die <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> sin, and rise <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> newness of life.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q75-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 75">s</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q75-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>n.</strong> Ezek. 36:27; Phil. 2:13; 2 Thess. 2:13; Eph. 1:4; 1 Cor. 6:11, <strong>o.</strong> Rom. 6:4-6; Col. 3:1-3; Phil. 3:10, <strong>p.</strong> 2 Cor. 5:17; Eph. 4:23-24; 1 Thess. 5:23, <strong>q.</strong> Acts 11:18; 1 John 3:9, <strong>r.</strong> Jude 20; Heb. 6:11-12; Eph. 3:16-19; Col. 1:10-11, <strong>s.</strong> Ezek. 36:25-27; Rom. 6:4, 6, 12-14; 2 Cor. 7:1; 1 Pet. 2:24; Gal. 5:24</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q76"></span>
### Question 76: What is repentance unto life?
Answer: Repentance unto life is a saving grace,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q76-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 76">t</a></sup> wrought in the heart of a sinner by the Spirit<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q76-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 76">u</a></sup> and Word of God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q76-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 76">w</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by which,">whereby,</span> out of the sight and sense, not only of the danger,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q76-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 76">x</a></sup> but also of the filthiness and odiousness of his sins,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q76-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 76">y</a></sup> and upon the apprehension of God’s mercy in Christ to <span class="text-variant" data-modern="those who">such</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">as</span> are penitent,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q76-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 76">z</a></sup> he so grieves for<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q76-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 76">a</a></sup> and hates his sins,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q76-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 76">b</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">as</span> that he turns from them all to God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q76-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 76">c</a></sup> purposing and endeavoring constantly to walk with him in all the ways of new obedience.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q76-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 76">d</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q76-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>t.</strong> 2 Tim. 2:25; Acts 11:18, <strong>u.</strong> Zech. 12:10, <strong>w.</strong> Acts 11:18, 20-21, <strong>x.</strong> Ezek. 18:28, 30, 32; Luke 15:17-18; Hos. 2:6-7, <strong>y.</strong> Ezek. 36:31; Isa. 30:22; Phil. 3:7-8, <strong>z.</strong> Joel 2:12-13; Ps. 51:1-4; Luke 15:7, 10; Acts 2:37, <strong>a.</strong> Jer. 31:18-19; Ps. 32:5, <strong>b.</strong> 2 Cor. 7:11, <strong>c.</strong> Luke 1:16-17; 1 Thess. 1:9; Acts 26:18; Ezek. 14:6; 1 Kings 8:47-48, <strong>d.</strong> 2 Chron. 7:14; Ps. 119:57-64; Matt. 3:8; 2 Cor. 7:10; Luke 1:6</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q77"></span>
### Question 77: <span class="text-variant" data-modern="How">Wherein</span> do justification and sanctification differ?
Answer: Although sanctification <span class="text-variant" data-modern="is">be</span> inseparably joined with justification,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q77-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 77">e</a></sup> yet they differ, in that God in justification <span class="text-variant" data-modern="imputes">imputeth</span> the righteousness of Christ;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q77-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 77">f</a></sup> in sanctification his Spirit <span class="text-variant" data-modern="infuses">infuseth</span> grace, and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="enables">enableth</span> to the exercise <span class="text-variant" data-modern="of it;">thereof;</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q77-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 77">g</a></sup> in the former, sin is pardoned;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q77-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 77">h</a></sup> in the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="latter,">other,</span> it is subdued:<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q77-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 77">i</a></sup> the one <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">doth</span> equally <span class="text-variant" data-modern="frees">free</span> all believers from the revenging wrath of God, and that perfectly in this life, that they never fall into condemnation;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q77-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 77">k</a></sup> the other is neither equal in all,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q77-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 77">l</a></sup> nor in this life perfect in any,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q77-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 77">m</a></sup> but growing up to perfection.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q77-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 77">n</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q77-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>e.</strong> 1 Cor. 6:11; 1 Cor. 1:30, <strong>f.</strong> Rom. 4:6, 8, <strong>g.</strong> Ezek. 36:27; Heb. 9:13-14, <strong>h.</strong> Rom. 3:24-25, <strong>i.</strong> Rom. 6:6, 14, <strong>k.</strong> Rom. 8:33-34, <strong>l.</strong> 1 John 2:12-14; Heb. 5:12-14, <strong>m.</strong> 1 John 1:8, 10, <strong>n.</strong> 2 Cor. 7:1; Phil. 3:12-14</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q78"></span>
### Question 78: <span class="text-variant" data-modern="From where does">Whence</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">ariseth</span> the imperfection of sanctification in <span class="text-variant" data-modern="believers arise?">believers?</span>
Answer: The imperfection of sanctification in believers <span class="text-variant" data-modern="arises">ariseth</span> from the remnants of sin abiding in every part of them, and the perpetual <span class="text-variant" data-modern="desires">lustings</span> of the flesh against the spirit; <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by which">whereby</span> they are often foiled with temptations, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">and</span> fall into many sins,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q78-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 78">o</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="and"></span> are hindered in all their spiritual services,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q78-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 78">p</a></sup> and their best works are imperfect and defiled in the sight of God.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q78-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 78">q</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q78-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>o.</strong> Rom. 7:18, 23; Mark 14:66-72; Gal. 2:11-12, <strong>p.</strong> Heb. 12:1, <strong>q.</strong> Isa. 64:6; Ex. 28:38; Gal. 5:16-18</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q79"></span>
### Question 79: May not true believers, by reason of their imperfections, and the many temptations and sins <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that overtake them,">they</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">are</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">overtaken</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">with,</span> fall away from the state of grace?
Answer: True believers, by reason of the unchangeable love of God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q79-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 79">r</a></sup> and his decree and covenant to give them perseverance,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q79-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 79">s</a></sup> their inseparable union with Christ,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q79-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 79">t</a></sup> his continual intercession for them,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q79-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 79">u</a></sup> and the Spirit and seed of God abiding in them,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q79-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 79">w</a></sup> can neither totally nor finally fall away from the state of grace,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q79-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 79">x</a></sup> but are kept by the power of God through faith <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> salvation.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q79-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 79">y</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q79-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>r.</strong> Jer. 31:3, <strong>s.</strong> 2 Tim. 2:19; Heb. 13:20-21; 2 Sam. 23:5, <strong>t.</strong> 1 Cor. 1:8-9, <strong>u.</strong> Heb. 7:25; Luke 22:32, <strong>w.</strong> 1 John 3:9; 1 John 2:27, <strong>x.</strong> Jer. 32:40; John 10:28, <strong>y.</strong> 1 Pet. 1:5</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q80"></span>
### Question 80: Can true believers be infallibly assured that they are in the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> of grace, and that they shall persevere <span class="text-variant" data-modern="in it to">therein</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">unto</span> salvation?
Answer: <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Those who">Such</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">as</span> truly believe in Christ, and endeavor to walk in all good conscience before him,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q80-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 80">z</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="may be infallibly assured that they are in the state of grace and shall persevere in it to salvation,">may,</span> without extraordinary revelation, by faith grounded upon the truth of God’s promises, and by the Spirit enabling them to discern in themselves those graces to which the promises of life are made,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q80-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 80">a</a></sup> and bearing witness with their spirits that they are the children of <span class="text-variant" data-modern="God.">God,</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q80-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 80">b</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">be</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">infallibly</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">assured</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">that</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">they</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">are</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">in</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">the</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">estate</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">of</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">grace,</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">and</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">shall</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">persevere</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">therein</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">unto</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">salvation.</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q80-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 80">c</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q80-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>z.</strong> 1 John 2:3; Heb. 10:19-23, <strong>a.</strong> 1 Cor. 2:12; 1 John 3:14, 18-19, 21, 24; 1 John 4:13, 16; Heb. 6:11-12, <strong>b.</strong> Rom. 8:15-16, <strong>c.</strong> 1 John 5:13; Heb. 6:19-20; 2 Pet. 1:5-11</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q81"></span>
### Question 81: Are all true believers at all times assured of their present being in the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> of grace, and that they shall be saved?
Answer: Assurance of grace and salvation not being of the essence of faith,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q81-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 81">d</a></sup> true believers may wait long before they obtain it;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q81-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 81">e</a></sup> and, after the enjoyment <span class="text-variant" data-modern="of it,">thereof,</span> may have it weakened and intermitted, through manifold <span class="text-variant" data-modern="ailments,">distempers,</span> sins, temptations, and desertions;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q81-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 81">f</a></sup> yet <span class="text-variant" data-modern="they"></span> are <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">they</span> never left without such a presence and support of the Spirit of God as keeps them from sinking into utter despair.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q81-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 81">g</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q81-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>d.</strong> Eph. 1:13, <strong>e.</strong> Isa. 50:10; Ps. 88:1-3, 6-7, 9-10, 13-15, <strong>f.</strong> Ps. 77:1-12; Ps. 51:8, 12; Ps. 31:22; Ps. 22:1; Eph. 4:30; Luke 22:31-34, <strong>g.</strong> 1 John 3:9; Ps. 73:15, 23; Isa. 54:7-10; 1 Pet. 4:12-14</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q82"></span>
### Question 82: What is the communion in glory <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> the members of the invisible church have with Christ?
Answer: The communion in glory <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> the members of the invisible church have with Christ, is in this life,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q82-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 82">h</a></sup> immediately after death,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q82-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 82">i</a></sup> and at last perfected at the resurrection and day of judgment.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q82-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 82">k</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q82-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>h.</strong> 2 Cor. 3:18, <strong>i.</strong> Luke 23:43, <strong>k.</strong> 1 Thess. 4:17</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q83"></span>
### Question 83: What is the communion in glory with Christ <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> the members of the invisible church enjoy in this life?
Answer: The members of the invisible church have communicated to them in this life the firstfruits of glory with Christ, as they are members of him their head, and so in him <span class="text-variant" data-modern="share">are</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">interested</span> in that glory which he <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">is</span> fully <span class="text-variant" data-modern="possesses;">possessed</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">of;</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q83-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 83">l</a></sup> and, as <span class="text-variant" data-modern="a guarantee of it,">an</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">earnest</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">thereof,</span> enjoy the sense of God’s love,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q83-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 83">m</a></sup> peace of conscience, joy in the Holy <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Spirit,">Ghost,</span> and hope of glory;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q83-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 83">n</a></sup> as, on the contrary, sense of God’s revenging wrath, horror of conscience, and a fearful expectation of judgment, are to the wicked the beginning of their <span class="text-variant" data-modern="torments,">torments</span> which they shall endure after death.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q83-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 83">o</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q83-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>l.</strong> Eph. 2:5-6, <strong>m.</strong> Rom. 5:5; 2 Cor. 1:22, <strong>n.</strong> Rom. 5:1-2; Rom. 14:17; 2 Pet. 3:18, <strong>o.</strong> Gen. 4:13; Matt. 27:4; Heb. 10:27; Rom. 2:9; Mark 9:44</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q84"></span>
### Question 84: Shall all men die?
Answer: Death being threatened as the wages of sin,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q84-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 84">p</a></sup> it is appointed <span class="text-variant" data-modern="for">unto</span> all men <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">once</span> to <span class="text-variant" data-modern="die once, because">die;</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q84-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 84">q</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">for</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">that</span> all have sinned.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q84-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 84">r</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q84-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>p.</strong> Rom. 6:23, <strong>q.</strong> Heb. 9:27, <strong>r.</strong> Rom. 5:12</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q85"></span>
### Question 85: Death being the wages of sin, why are not the righteous delivered from death, seeing all their sins are forgiven in Christ?
Answer: The righteous shall be delivered from death itself at the last day, and even in death are delivered from the sting and curse of <span class="text-variant" data-modern="it;">it</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q85-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 85">s</a></sup> so that, although they die, yet it is out of God’s love,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q85-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 85">t</a></sup> to free them perfectly from sin and misery,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q85-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 85">u</a></sup> and to make them capable of further communion with Christ in glory, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="into"></span> which they then <span class="text-variant" data-modern="enter.">enter</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">upon.</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q85-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 85">w</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q85-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>s.</strong> 1 Cor. 15:26, 55-57; Heb. 2:15; John 11:25-26, <strong>t.</strong> Isa. 57:1-2; 2 Kings 22:20, <strong>u.</strong> Rev. 14:13; Eph. 5:27, <strong>w.</strong> Luke 23:43; Phil. 1:23</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q86"></span>
### Question 86: What is the communion in glory with <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Christ that">Christ,</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">which</span> the members of the invisible church enjoy immediately after death?
Answer: The communion in glory with <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Christ that">Christ,</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">which</span> the members of the invisible church enjoy immediately after death, is, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">in</span> that their souls are then made perfect in holiness,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q86-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 86">x</a></sup> and received into the highest heavens,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q86-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 86">y</a></sup> where they behold the face of God in light and glory,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q86-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 86">z</a></sup> waiting for the full redemption of their bodies,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q86-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 86">a</a></sup> which even in death continue united to Christ,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q86-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 86">b</a></sup> and rest in their graves as in their beds,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q86-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 86">c</a></sup> till at the last day they <span class="text-variant" data-modern="are">be</span> again united to their souls.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q86-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 86">d</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="However,">Whereas</span> the souls of the wicked are at their death cast into hell, where they remain in torments and utter darkness, and their bodies kept in their graves, as in their prisons, till the resurrection and judgment of the great day.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q86-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 86">e</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q86-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>x.</strong> Heb. 12:23; Acts 7:55, 59, <strong>y.</strong> 2 Cor. 5:1, 6, 8; Phil. 1:23; Acts 3:21; Eph. 4:10; Luke 23:43, <strong>z.</strong> 1 John 3:2; 1 Cor. 13:12, <strong>a.</strong> Rom. 8:23; Ps. 16:9, <strong>b.</strong> 1 Thess. 4:14, 16, <strong>c.</strong> Isa. 57:2, <strong>d.</strong> Job 19:26-27, <strong>e.</strong> Luke 16:23-24; Acts 1:25; Jude 6</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q87"></span>
### Question 87: What are we to believe concerning the resurrection?
Answer: We are to <span class="text-variant" data-modern="believe">believe,</span> that at the last day there shall be a general resurrection of the dead, both of the just and unjust:<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q87-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 87">f</a></sup> when <span class="text-variant" data-modern="those who">they</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">that</span> are then found alive shall in a moment be changed; and the selfsame bodies of the dead <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> were laid in the grave, being then again united to their souls forever, shall be raised up by the power of Christ.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q87-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 87">g</a></sup> The bodies of the just, by the Spirit of Christ, and by virtue of his resurrection as their head, shall be raised in power, spiritual, incorruptible, and made like <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">to</span> his glorious body;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q87-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 87">h</a></sup> and the bodies of the wicked shall be raised up in dishonor by him, as an offended judge.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q87-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 87">i</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q87-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>f.</strong> Dan. 12:2; Acts 24:15, <strong>g.</strong> Job 19:26; 1 Cor. 15:51-53; 1 Thess. 4:15-17; John 5:28-29; Rom. 8:11, <strong>h.</strong> 1 Cor. 15:21-23, 42-44; Phil. 3:21, <strong>i.</strong> John 5:27-29; Matt. 25:33</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q88"></span>
### Question 88: What shall immediately follow after the resurrection?
Answer: Immediately after the resurrection shall follow the general and final judgment of angels and men;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q88-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 88">k</a></sup> the day and hour <span class="text-variant" data-modern="of which">whereof</span> no man <span class="text-variant" data-modern="knows,">knoweth,</span> that all may watch and pray, and be ever ready for the coming of the Lord.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q88-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 88">l</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q88-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>k.</strong> Eccl. 12:14; 2 Pet. 2:4, 6-7, 14-15; Matt. 25:46; 2 Cor. 5:10; Rom. 14:10, 12, <strong>l.</strong> Matt. 24:36, 42, 44; Mark 13:35-37</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q89"></span>
### Question 89: What shall be done to the wicked at the day of judgment?
Answer: At the day of judgment, the wicked shall be set on Christ’s left hand,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q89-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 89">m</a></sup> and, upon clear evidence, and full conviction of their own consciences,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q89-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 89">n</a></sup> shall have the fearful but just sentence of condemnation pronounced against them;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q89-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 89">o</a></sup> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="therefore">thereupon</span> shall <span class="text-variant" data-modern="then"></span> be cast out from the favorable presence of God, and the glorious fellowship with Christ, his saints, and all his holy angels, into hell, to be punished with unspeakable torments, both of body and soul, with the devil and his angels forever.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q89-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 89">p</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q89-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>m.</strong> Matt. 25:33, <strong>n.</strong> Rom. 2:15-16, <strong>o.</strong> Matt. 25:41-43, <strong>p.</strong> Luke 16:26; 2 Thess. 1:8-9</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q90"></span>
### Question 90: What shall be done to the righteous at the day of judgment?
Answer: At the day of judgment, the righteous, being caught up to Christ in the clouds,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q90-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 90">q</a></sup> shall be set on his right hand, and there openly acknowledged and acquitted,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q90-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 90">r</a></sup> shall join with him in the judging of reprobate angels and men,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q90-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 90">s</a></sup> and shall be received into heaven,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q90-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 90">t</a></sup> where they shall be fully and forever freed from all sin and misery;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q90-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 90">u</a></sup> filled with inconceivable joys,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q90-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 90">w</a></sup> made perfectly holy and happy both in body and soul, in the company of innumerable saints and holy angels,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q90-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 90">x</a></sup> but especially in the immediate vision and fruition of God the Father, of our Lord Jesus Christ, and of the Holy Spirit, to all eternity.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q90-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 90">y</a></sup> And this is the perfect and full communion, which the members of the invisible church shall enjoy with Christ in glory, at the resurrection and day of judgment. HAVING SEEN WHAT THE SCRIPTURES PRINCIPALLY TEACH US BG, IF TO ELIEVE CONCERNING ODT OLLOWS TO CONSIDER WHAT THEY REQUIRE AS THE DUTY OF MAN

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q90-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>q.</strong> 1 Thess. 4:17; 1 Cor. 15:42-43, <strong>r.</strong> Matt. 25:33; Matt. 10:32, <strong>s.</strong> 1 Cor. 6:2-3, <strong>t.</strong> Matt. 25:34, 46, <strong>u.</strong> Eph. 5:27; Rev. 14:13, <strong>w.</strong> Ps. 16:11, <strong>x.</strong> Heb. 12:22-23, <strong>y.</strong> 1 John 3:2; Rom. 8:29; 1 Cor. 13:12; 1 Thess. 4:17-18</p>
</div>
</details>


## HAVING SEEN WHAT THE SCRIPTURES PRINCIPALLY TEACH US TO BELIEVE CONCERNING GOD, IT FOLLOWS TO CONSIDER WHAT THEY REQUIRE AS THE DUTY OF MAN

<span id="wlc-q91"></span>
### Question 91: What is the duty <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> God <span class="text-variant" data-modern="requires">requireth</span> of man?
Answer: The duty <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> God <span class="text-variant" data-modern="requires">requireth</span> of man, is obedience to his revealed will.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q91-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 91">z</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q91-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>z.</strong> Deut. 29:29; Mic. 6:8; 1 John 5:2-3; Rom. 12:1-2</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q92"></span>
### Question 92: What did God at first reveal <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> man as the rule of his obedience?
Answer: The rule of obedience revealed to Adam in the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> of innocence, and to all mankind in him, besides a special command not to eat of the fruit of the tree of the knowledge of good and evil, was the moral law.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q92-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 92">a</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q92-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>a.</strong> Gen. 1:26-27; Rom. 2:14-15; Rom. 10:5; Gen. 2:17</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q93"></span>
### Question 93: What is the moral law?
Answer: The moral law is the declaration of the will of God to mankind, directing and binding everyone to personal, perfect, and perpetual conformity and obedience <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to it,">thereunto,</span> in the frame and disposition of the whole man, soul and body,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q93-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 93">b</a></sup> and in performance of all those duties of holiness and righteousness <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> he <span class="text-variant" data-modern="owes">oweth</span> to God and man:<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q93-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 93">c</a></sup> promising life upon the fulfilling, and threatening death upon the breach of it.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q93-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 93">d</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q93-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>b.</strong> Deut. 5:1-3, 31, 33; Luke 10:26-27; 1 Thess. 5:23; Eph. 4:24, <strong>c.</strong> Luke 1:75; Acts 24:16; 1 Pet. 1:15-16, <strong>d.</strong> Rom. 10:5; Gal. 3:10, 12; Rom. 5:12</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q94"></span>
### Question 94: Is there any use of the moral law to man since the fall?
Answer: Although no man, since the fall, can attain to righteousness and life by the moral law;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q94-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 94">e</a></sup> yet there is great use <span class="text-variant" data-modern="of it,">thereof,</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">as</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">well</span> common to all men, as <span class="text-variant" data-modern="well as particular">peculiar</span> either to the unregenerate, or the regenerate.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q94-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 94">f</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q94-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>e.</strong> Rom. 8:3; Gal. 2:16, <strong>f.</strong> 1 Tim. 1:8</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q95"></span>
### Question 95: Of what use is the moral law to all men?
Answer: The moral law is of use to all men, to inform them of the holy nature and will of God,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q95-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 95">g</a></sup> and of their duty, binding them to walk accordingly;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q95-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 95">h</a></sup> to convince them of their disability to keep it, and of the sinful pollution of their nature, hearts, and lives; to humble them in the sense of their sin and misery,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q95-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 95">k</a></sup> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="by this">thereby</span> help them to a clearer sight of the need they have of Christ,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q95-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 95">l</a></sup> and of the perfection of his obedience.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q95-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 95">m</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q95-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>g.</strong> Rom. 1:20; Lev. 11:44-45; Lev. 20:7-8; Rom. 7:12, <strong>h.</strong> Mic. 6:8; James 2:10-11; Rom. 1:32, <strong>i.</strong> Ps. 19:11-12; Rom. 3:20; Rom. 7:7, <strong>k.</strong> Rom. 3:9, 23, <strong>l.</strong> Gal. 3:21-22, 24, <strong>m.</strong> Rom. 10:4</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q96"></span>
### Question 96: What particular use is there of the moral law to unregenerate men?
Answer: The moral law is of use to unregenerate men, to awaken their consciences to flee from <span class="text-variant" data-modern="the"></span> wrath to come,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q96-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 96">n</a></sup> and to drive them to Christ;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q96-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 96">o</a></sup> or, upon their continuance in the <span class="text-variant" data-modern="state">estate</span> and way of sin, to leave them inexcusable,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q96-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 96">p</a></sup> and under <span class="text-variant" data-modern="its curse.">the</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">curse</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">thereof.</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q96-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 96">q</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q96-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>n.</strong> Ps. 51:13; 1 Tim. 1:9-11, <strong>o.</strong> Gal. 3:24, <strong>p.</strong> Rom. 1:20; Rom. 2:15, <strong>q.</strong> Gal. 3:10</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q97"></span>
### Question 97: What special use is there of the moral law to the regenerate?
Answer: Although <span class="text-variant" data-modern="those who">they</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">that</span> are regenerate, and believe in Christ, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="are">be</span> delivered from the moral law as a covenant of works,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q97-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 97">r</a></sup> so <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">as</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">thereby</span> they are neither justified<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q97-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 97">s</a></sup> nor <span class="text-variant" data-modern="condemned by it;">condemned;</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q97-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 97">t</a></sup> yet, besides <span class="text-variant" data-modern="its">the</span> general uses <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">thereof</span> common to them with all men, it is of special use, to show them how much they are bound to Christ for his fulfilling it, and enduring <span class="text-variant" data-modern="its">the</span> curse <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">thereof</span> in their <span class="text-variant" data-modern="place,">stead,</span> and for their good;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q97-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 97">u</a></sup> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">thereby</span> to <span class="text-variant" data-modern="arouse">provoke</span> them <span class="text-variant" data-modern="in this way"></span> to more thankfulness,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q97-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 97">w</a></sup> and to express the same in their greater care to conform themselves <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to it">thereunto</span> as the rule of their obedience.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q97-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 97">x</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q97-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>r.</strong> Rom. 6:14; Rom. 7:4, 6; Gal. 4:4-5; Col. 2:13-14, <strong>s.</strong> Rom. 3:20, <strong>t.</strong> Gal. 5:23; Rom. 8:1, <strong>u.</strong> Rom. 7:24-25; Gal. 3:13-14; Rom. 8:3-4; Acts 13:38-39, <strong>w.</strong> Luke 1:68-69, 74-75; Col. 1:12-14; Rom. 6:14, <strong>x.</strong> Deut. 30:19-20; Rom. 7:22; Rom. 12:2; Titus 2:11-14; James 1:25</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q98"></span>
### Question 98: Where is the moral law <span class="text-variant" data-modern="summarized?">summarily</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">comprehended?</span>
Answer: The moral law is <span class="text-variant" data-modern="summarized">summarily</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">comprehended</span> in the Ten Commandments, which were delivered by the voice of God upon Mount Sinai, and written by him in two tables of stone;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q98-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 98">y</a></sup> and are recorded in the twentieth chapter of Exodus: the four first commandments containing our duty to God, and the other six our duty to man.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q98-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 98">z</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q98-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>y.</strong> Deut. 4:13; Deut. 10:4; Ex. 34:1-4; Rom. 13:8-10; James 2:8, 10-12, <strong>z.</strong> Matt. 22:37-40; Matt. 19:17-19</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q99"></span>
### Question 99: What rules are to be observed for the right understanding of the Ten Commandments?
Answer: For the right understanding of the Ten Commandments, these rules are to be observed: 1. That the law is perfect, and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="binds">bindeth</span> everyone to full conformity in the whole man <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to its righteousness,">unto</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">the</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">righteousness</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">thereof,</span> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to">unto</span> entire obedience forever; so as to require the utmost perfection of every duty, and to forbid the least degree of every sin.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 99">a</a></sup> 2. That it is spiritual, and so <span class="text-variant" data-modern="reaches">reacheth</span> the understanding, will, affections, and all other powers of the soul; as well as words, works, and gestures.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 99">b</a></sup> 3. That one and the same thing, in <span class="text-variant" data-modern="different">divers</span> respects, is required or forbidden in several commandments.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 99">c</a></sup> 4. That <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">as,</span> where a duty is commanded, the contrary sin is forbidden;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 99">d</a></sup> and, where a sin is forbidden, the contrary duty is <span class="text-variant" data-modern="commanded; likewise,">commanded:</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">so,</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 99">e</a></sup> where a promise is annexed, the contrary threatening is included;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 99">f</a></sup> and, where a threatening is annexed, the contrary promise is included.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 99">g</a></sup> 5. That what God forbids, is at no time to be done;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 99">h</a></sup> what he commands, is always our duty;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 99">i</a></sup> and yet every particular duty is not to be done at all times.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 99">k</a></sup> 6. That under one sin or duty, all of the same kind are forbidden or commanded; together with all the causes, means, occasions, and appearances <span class="text-variant" data-modern="of it,">thereof,</span> and provocations <span class="text-variant" data-modern="to it.">thereunto.</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 99">l</a></sup> 7. That what is forbidden or commanded to ourselves, we are bound, according to our places, to endeavor that it may be avoided or performed by others, according to the duty of their places.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 99">m</a></sup> 8. That in what is commanded to others, we are bound, according to our places and callings, to be helpful to them;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 99">n</a></sup> and to take heed of partaking with others in what is forbidden them.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q99-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 99">o</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q99-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>a.</strong> Ps. 19:7; James 2:10; Matt. 5:21-22, <strong>b.</strong> Rom. 7:14; Deut. 6:5; Matt. 22:37-39; Matt. 5:21-22, 27-28, 33-34, 37-39, 43-44, <strong>c.</strong> Col. 3:5; Amos 8:5; Prov. 1:19; 1 Tim. 6:10, <strong>d.</strong> Isa. 58:13; Deut. 6:13; Matt. 4:9-10; Matt. 15:4-6, <strong>e.</strong> Matt. 5:21-25; Eph. 4:28, <strong>f.</strong> Ex. 20:12; Prov. 30:17, <strong>g.</strong> Jer. 18:7-8; Ex. 20:7; Ps. 15:1, 4-5; Ps. 24:4-5, <strong>h.</strong> Job 13:7-8; Rom. 3:8; Job 36:21; Heb. 11:25, <strong>i.</strong> Deut. 4:8-9; Luke 17:10, <strong>k.</strong> Matt. 12:7, <strong>l.</strong> Matt. 5:21-22, 27-28; Matt. 15:4-6; 1 Thess. 5:22; Jude 23; Gal. 5:26; Col. 3:21, <strong>m.</strong> Ex. 20:10; Lev. 19:17; Gen. 18:19; Josh. 24:15; Deut. 6:6-7; Heb. 10:24-25, <strong>n.</strong> 2 Cor. 1:24, <strong>o.</strong> 1 Tim. 5:22; Eph. 5:11</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q100"></span>
### Question 100: What special things are we to consider in the Ten Commandments?
Answer: We are to consider, in the Ten Commandments, the preface, the substance of the commandments themselves, and several reasons annexed to some of them, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">the</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">more</span> to enforce <span class="text-variant" data-modern="them even more.">them.</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q100-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 100">p</a></sup><sup class="proof-marker">p</sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q100-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>p.</strong> Eph. 6:1-3</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q101"></span>
### Question 101: What is the preface to the Ten Commandments?
Answer: The preface to the Ten Commandments is contained in these words, I am the LORD <span class="text-variant" data-modern="your">thy</span> God, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="who">which</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">have</span> brought <span class="text-variant" data-modern="you">thee</span> out of the land of Egypt, out of the house of <span class="text-variant" data-modern="slavery. In this">bondage.</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q101-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 101">q</a></sup> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">Wherein</span> God <span class="text-variant" data-modern="manifests">manifesteth</span> his sovereignty, as being <span class="text-variant" data-modern="Yahweh,">JEHOVAH,</span> the eternal, immutable, and almighty God;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q101-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 101">r</a></sup> having his being in and of himself,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q101-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 101">s</a></sup> and giving being to all his words<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q101-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 101">t</a></sup> and works:<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q101-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 101">u</a></sup> and that he is a God in covenant, as with Israel of old, so with all his people;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q101-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 101">w</a></sup> who, as he brought them out of their bondage in Egypt, so he <span class="text-variant" data-modern="delivers">delivereth</span> us from our spiritual <span class="text-variant" data-modern="slavery;">thraldom;</span><sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q101-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 101">x</a></sup> and that therefore we are bound to take him for our God alone, and to keep all his commandments.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q101-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 101">y</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q101-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>q.</strong> Ex. 20:2; Deut. 5:6, <strong>r.</strong> Isa. 44:6, <strong>s.</strong> Ex. 3:14, <strong>t.</strong> Ex. 6:3, <strong>u.</strong> Acts 17:24, 28, <strong>w.</strong> Gen. 17:7; Rom. 3:29, <strong>x.</strong> Luke 1:74-75; Gal. 5:1, <strong>y.</strong> 1 Pet. 1:15-19; Lev. 18:30; Lev. 19:37</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q102"></span>
### Question 102: What is the sum of the four commandments <span class="text-variant" data-modern="that">which</span> contain our duty to God?
Answer: The sum of the four commandments containing our duty to <span class="text-variant" data-modern="God,">God</span> is, to love the Lord our God with all our heart, and with all our soul, and with all our strength, and with all our mind.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q102-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 102">z</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q102-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>z.</strong> Luke 10:27; Matt. 22:37-40</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q103"></span>
### Question 103: Which is the first commandment?
Answer: The first commandment is, <span class="text-variant" data-modern="You shall">Thou</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">shalt</span> have no other gods before me.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q103-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof a, question 103">a</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q103-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
<p><strong>a.</strong> Ex. 20:3; Deut. 5:7</p>
</div>
</details>


<span id="wlc-q104"></span>
### Question 104: What are the duties required in the first commandment?
Answer: The duties required in the first commandment <span class="text-variant" data-modern="are to know">are,</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">the</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">knowing</span> and <span class="text-variant" data-modern="acknowledge">acknowledging</span> <span class="text-variant" data-modern="">of</span> God to be the only true God, and our God;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof b, question 104">b</a></sup> and to worship and glorify him accordingly,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof c, question 104">c</a></sup> by thinking,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof d, question 104">d</a></sup> meditating,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof e, question 104">e</a></sup> remembering,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof f, question 104">f</a></sup> highly esteeming,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof g, question 104">g</a></sup> honoring,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof h, question 104">h</a></sup> adoring,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof i, question 104">i</a></sup> choosing,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof k, question 104">k</a></sup> loving,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof l, question 104">l</a></sup> desiring,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof m, question 104">m</a></sup> fearing of him;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof n, question 104">n</a></sup> believing him;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof o, question 104">o</a></sup> trusting,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof p, question 104">p</a></sup> hoping,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof q, question 104">q</a></sup> delighting,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof r, question 104">r</a></sup> rejoicing in him;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof s, question 104">s</a></sup> being zealous for him;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof t, question 104">t</a></sup> calling upon him, giving all praise and thanks,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof u, question 104">u</a></sup> and yielding all obedience and submission to him with the whole man;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof w, question 104">w</a></sup> being careful in all things to please him,<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof x, question 104">x</a></sup> and sorrowful when in anything he is offended;<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof y, question 104">y</a></sup> and walking humbly with him.<sup class="proof-marker"><a href="#wlc-q104-proofs" aria-label="Scripture proof z, question 104">z</a></sup>

<details class="scripture-proofs" id="wlc-q104-proofs">
<summary>Scripture Proofs</summary>
<div class="proofs-body">
